And what shift was that?

It was four to midnight, but I was scheduled to work till 8:00 PM.

8:00 PM?

Yes, ma'am.

Attorney Sprague ([29:41](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1781.85)):

That is correct.

Attorney Sprague ([29:46](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1786.26)):

And you were in a cruiser, in a marked cruiser and a uniform?

Yes. I was fully dressed in Duxbury Police uniform. I was in a marked cruiser, Duxbury Police 172.

Attorney Sprague ([29:59](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1799.28)):

And did you receive a dispatch at approximately 6:11 PM to go to 47 Summer Street in Duxbury?

Brian Josephine ([30:04](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1804.95)):

Yes, we did.

Attorney Sprague ([30:06](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1806.12)):

And when you say we, were you with a partner or were you alone?

Brian Josephine ([30:09](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1809.09)):

No, I was by myself. We ride single in a cruiser there.

Attorney Sprague ([30:12](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1812.72)):

Okay. So when you say we, do you mean that it was dispatched to all officers on patrol?

Brian Josephine ([30:16](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1816.95)):

So  usually with critical incidents calls, they put it over to dispatch and  they'll say, Duxbury Police cars, start making your way to a certain  address. That way everyone can get there as quickly as possible.

Attorney Sprague ([30:30](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1830.36)):

Okay. And so then you knew at that point to go to 47 Summer Street?

Brian Josephine ([30:31](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1831.44)):

Yes, ma'am.

Attorney Sprague ([30:32](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1832.22)):

And approximately how long did it take you to get there?

I would say anywhere from three to four minutes.

Attorney Sprague ([30:37](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1837.29)):

So you were relatively close by when you received the dispatch?

Brian Josephine ([30:39](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1839.93)):

Yes, I was.

Attorney Sprague ([30:41](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1841.61)):

Okay. And when you arrived at 47 Summer Street, what did you observe?

Brian Josephine ([30:47](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1847.97)):

Dispatch  was giving me description of the house. It took probably 15 seconds for  me to identify the house. Parked in the street. I saw the number of the  house and I made my way. I believe it was a mudroom that I went to.

Attorney Sprague ([31:01](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1861.44)):

Showing  you Exhibit 33. When you say the mudroom that you went to, did you  actually go inside the house or did you go to the door to the mudroom?

Brian Josephine ([31:16](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1876.68)):

I went to the door to the mudroom to check if that was unlocked so I could make my way to the back of the house.

Attorney Sprague ([31:22](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1882.26)):

And is that door depicted in this photograph?

Brian Josephine ([31:24](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1884.96)):

Yes, it is.

Attorney Sprague ([31:25](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1885.92)):

And is it the door to the left of the garage?

Brian Josephine ([31:27](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1887.9)):

Yes, it is.

Attorney Sprague ([31:31](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1891.86)):

And was the door locked or unlocked?

Brian Josephine ([31:32](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1892.01)):

It was locked at that time.

Attorney Sprague ([31:33](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1893.24)):

So what did you do next?

Brian Josephine ([31:35](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1895.04)):

I  radioed dispatch just for confirmation of where that party we were  trying to get to was located. They told me it was around back of the  house, so I tried to make my way that way.

Attorney Sprague ([31:45](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1905.24)):

And when you went to the back of the house, how did you get there?

Brian Josephine ([31:49](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1909.53)):

Well,  referring back to the picture where the car was parked, I went to the  right side. And I went around the garage. There was a fence and I went  over the fence, I believe.

Attorney Sprague ([31:58](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1918.98)):

So showing you Exhibit 33, again, you went by these cars and around the house to the right?

Brian Josephine ([32:04](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=1924.74)):

Shanan Buckingham ([37:02](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2222.58)):

We  immediately were trying to figure out where they were coming from. I'm  not sure if dispatch or something came over the air and said something  about the basement, but we both just started making our way back towards  the deck part of the house.

Shanan Buckingham ([37:20](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2240.94)):

Yes.

Shanan Buckingham ([37:29](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2249.4)):

Yes, it was the exact screaming that I heard from the window.

Shanan Buckingham ([37:38](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2258.7)):

Okay. And did you go down the stairs to the basement?

Yes, we did.

Shanan Buckingham ([37:45](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2265.33)):

And did you see anything after you went down the stairs?

Brian Josephine ([37:48](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2268.99)):

So  we went down the stairs and there was a picture at the bottom of the  stairs, I believe, of Lindsay and Patrick. And the basement splits off  into two, a right and a left area. And the screams were coming from the  right area. So that's where we directed our attention to first.

Shanan Buckingham ([38:04](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2284.26)):

Yes.  That right side opens up and it's like two rooms, I believe. So we went  into the first room, which was kind of dark, and there was some  cluttered stuff around, and Patrick was in that back room at the time.

Shanan Buckingham ([38:35](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2315.1)):

Yes, it is.

Brian Josephine ([38:47](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2327.46)):

Yes, it is.

Shanan Buckingham ([38:51](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2331.51)):

And do you recall what, if anything, Patrick was saying as you encountered him?

Brian Josephine ([38:56](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2336.49)):

Upon  his observation of us, myself and Officer Hall, he proceeded to head in  our direction, leaving that room. And at that point he looked up and he  screamed, "She killed the fucking kids."

Shanan Buckingham ([39:10](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2350.89)):

And after he said that, where did you go?

Once  he cleared himself from the doorframe, we were able to look inside the  room where we observed what appeared to be a child laying motionless on  the floor.

Shanan Buckingham ([39:24](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2364.45)):

And did you later learn his name was Dawson Clancy?

Yes, I did.

Shanan Buckingham ([39:28](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2368.35)):

And how was he positioned?

Yes.

And so you're saying that he was in that area when you first saw him?

Yes, he was.

Shanan Buckingham ([39:55](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2395.44)):

And do you recall whether he was on his back or his stomach or something else?

Brian Josephine ([39:58](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2398.98)):

He was on his back.

Shanan Buckingham ([40:00](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2400.75)):

And do you recall his physical appearance?

Brian Josephine ([40:04](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2404.62)):

Like  I said, he was laying on his back. His head was tilted to the  right-hand side. Looked at him kind of in the face area once we got  closer and his face was white, gray. Lips were blue. Eyes were  bloodshot. Looked like blood vessels were popped in his eyes.

Shanan Buckingham ([40:25](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2425.32)):

Did you notice any injuries to his neck?

Brian Josephine ([40:28](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2428.08)):

Seeing  that, my first instinct was to kind of grab his head and tilt it to the  side, see if I can get a pulse. But, I mean, based on my training and  experience from previous incidents, I knew it wasn't going to be good.  So I moved the head. That's where I observed some red marks on that  right-hand side.

Shanan Buckingham ([40:45](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2445.72)):

Of the neck?

Brian Josephine ([40:45](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2445.93)):

Yes.

Shanan Buckingham ([40:46](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2446.32)):

And in that Exhibit 94, there was a yellow exercise band, correct?

Yes.

Shanan Buckingham ([40:51](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2451.39)):

And I believe in prior testimony, you may have called it a blue exercise band. Is that correct?

Yes.

Shanan Buckingham ([41:02](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2462.61)):

Yes, it's clearly yellow. I mean, there was-

[inaudible 00:41:07] color of the exercise band.

[inaudible 00:41:09]. All right, go ahead.

Shanan Buckingham ([41:10](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2470.53)):

So what was your focus at the time?

Brian Josephine ([41:12](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2472.42)):

The child.

Shanan Buckingham ([41:13](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2473.71)):

And once you saw him there with these injuries, what did you do?

Brian Josephine ([41:22](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2482.98)):

Based  on my observations, I knew there wasn't really much I could do. So I  picked the child up, went chest to chest. And as I did that, it was kind  of like a mucus or a foam that leaked out of his mouth onto me. I  grabbed him, went back towards the way. We came down into the basement  and I headed up to the stairs where Sergeant Homestead was coming down  the stairs and I started yelling to him to get out of the way. Went back  up the stairs and then into the kitchen directly adjacent to the stairs  is the front foyer hallway, and I went out that, out the main door into  the driveway.

Shanan Buckingham ([42:04](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2524.53)):

And was there an ambulance in the driveway at that time?

Brian Josephine ([42:07](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2527.23)):

Yes.  The ambulance was backing up and had pretty much just parked and one of  the fire was walking around to the stretcher out back. So I rushed over  with Dawson and I put him on the stretcher and that's when Sergeant  Homestead and fire personnel started working further on him.

Shanan Buckingham ([42:26](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2546.46)):

What did you do next?

Brian Josephine ([42:28](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2548.35)):

I went back into the house.

Shanan Buckingham ([42:29](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2549.55)):

And why did you go back into the house?

Brian Josephine ([42:31](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2551.83)):

I remember hearing something about kids and multiple and I just went back.

Shanan Buckingham ([42:37](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2557.11)):

And when you went back at the house, did you go to the basement?

Brian Josephine ([42:39](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2559.99)):

Yes, I went back in that front door, back down to the basement, and this time I went to the left-hand side.

Shanan Buckingham ([42:45](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2565.09)):

And when you went to the left-hand side of the basement, what did you see?

Brian Josephine ([42:49](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2569.68)):

Pretty much directly in my sight was an older female laying on the ground and then in the corner there was a younger baby.

Shanan Buckingham ([43:00](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2580.63)):

And when you say an older female, you're referring to that she was older than Dawson?

Brian Josephine ([43:04](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2584.14)):

That's correct.

Shanan Buckingham ([43:05](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2585.28)):

And did you later learn her name was Cora Clancy?

Brian Josephine ([43:08](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2588.88)):

I did.

Shanan Buckingham ([43:09](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2589.81)):

And did you assist in treating her?

Brian Josephine ([43:12](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2592.93)):

Yes, I did.

Shanan Buckingham ([43:13](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2593.68)):

What did you do?

Brian Josephine ([43:15](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2595.21)):

So  when I got down to the basement, two fire personnel were already on  scene. One was working on the baby and one was working on Cora.  Communicated with them. We worked together and I took over doing chest  compressions of Cora.

Shanan Buckingham ([43:33](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2613.66)):

And were you able to see any injuries on Cora at that time?

Brian Josephine ([43:37](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2617.56)):

Yes.  She had kind of the same injuries that I explained with Dawson. The  pale tint, bluish lips, bloodshot eyes, blood vessels popped. But this  time she also had dried blood around her mouth.

Shanan Buckingham ([43:55](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2635.14)):

And did you also see an area of blood near her on the floor?

Brian Josephine ([44:00](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2640.27)):

Yes,  there was a relatively large area of blood on the floor. Actually, when  doing CPR on her [inaudible 00:44:08] and that blood seeped through my  pants, so I ended up moving her a little off to the side so I could  position myself better.

Shanan Buckingham ([44:18](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2658.6)):

Just showing you what's been marked as Exhibit 97. This red-brown stain on the rug. Is that what you're referring to?

Brian Josephine ([44:25](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2665.38)):

Yes.

Shanan Buckingham ([44:35](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2675.04)):

Okay. While you were doing chest compressions on Cora, what was the firefighter doing?

Brian Josephine ([44:40](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2680.65)):

The one that had previously been attending to Cora then went to the child that was in the corner.

Shanan Buckingham ([44:48](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2688.45)):

And, eventually, was Cora put on a stretcher and brought out?

Brian Josephine ([44:57](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2697.99)):

Yes,  eventually. I think the baby was brought out first because I think they  mentioned at some point they might've found a faint pulse or something  like that, so they got him out first and then we were able to get Cora  out.

Shanan Buckingham ([45:12](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2712.09)):

So that entire time while you're in the basement, are you still giving chest compressions to Cora?

Brian Josephine ([45:17](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2717.46)):

Yes. Yeah. I mean, it felt like forever to the point where I think I radioed for assistance for another officer to come down.

Shanan Buckingham ([45:27](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2727.54)):

And did others come down to assist?

Brian Josephine ([45:29](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2729.82)):

Yes.  Officer Cahill came down to further assist where I continued with chest  compressions and he used a bag valve mask to give rescue breaths.

Shanan Buckingham ([45:41](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2741.49)):

And  a bag valve mask, is that something that goes over someone's face, a  mask that's attached to a bag and you squeeze it to give air?

Brian Josephine ([45:48](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2748.09)):

Yes, it's an alternative breathing apparatus.

Shanan Buckingham ([45:53](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2753.1)):

Okay. At some point, do you go back upstairs to the first floor?

Brian Josephine ([45:57](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2757.81)):

Yes.

Shanan Buckingham ([45:59](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2759.22)):

And do you encounter Patrick Clancy when you go upstairs?

Brian Josephine ([46:03](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2763.66)):

Yes. So when they removed Cora, myself and Officer Cahill went back upstairs where I encountered Mr. Clancy in the kitchen.

Shanan Buckingham ([46:13](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2773.95)):

And what was his demeanor in the kitchen?

Brian Josephine ([46:17](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2777.16)):

He  was still frantic, kind of in shock, still screaming and losing  himself. And I think at that point, I don't know, one of us requested  that we get an ambulance for him as well.

Shanan Buckingham ([46:31](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2791.14)):

Thank you, sir. Nothing further.

Judge ([46:32](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=2792.37)):

Attorney Reddington?

Because  of the severity of Ms. Clancy on the ground, and now we have another  patient, I put a all call in, which is a recall of Duxbury personnel,  all Duxbury personnel, if they're available, to come back to the town to  help out for when I need help on the scene. And I also need help in the  town to cover the town while I'm busy at this scene. And I also  requested a Mutual Aid ambulance to respond to the scene.

Mutual  Aid, it's a shared system that all the towns use, that if we get busy  or busy with calls, we are able to call help in from surrounding towns  to give us a hand, whether it be fire-related or medical-related.

Shanan Buckingham ([55:58](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=3358.83)):

I  was an EMT, which 80% of our calls are probably that related. So I  would assist a paramedic or I would drive the ambulance or whatever was  necessary at the time. And then we also did fire suppression and we were  all trained in that and went to the academy and all that.

Ms. Buckingham ([59:46](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=3586.92)):

So  a paramedic requires another year or two of training and they're able  to prescribe meds. I mean, not prescribe meds, excuse me, give meds that  they have according to what they need to do. They can also read the  monitor that shows rhythms in the heart and what have you.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:00:11](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=3611.58)):

Is  it fair to say that as an EMT, you're trained in basic lifesaving  techniques while a paramedic is advanced lifesaving techniques?

Correct.

So  at that time, because we had heard what kind of call, we had an idea of  what call we were going to, the two medics sat up front and were able  to converse while I sat in the back and again was that backup guy, more  or less.

Correct.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:04:37](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=3877.68)):

Not  at that point. Because I was in the back, I switched to the driver's  seat. And at that time I saw an officer coming and he was getting ready  to move the vehicle that was in our way so that I could back in.

Correct.

What did you see?

At that point, no.

I believe I worked maybe two days prior. I don't really remember.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:13:23](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4403.67)):

Okay. So, you were on a day off?

Keith Nette ([01:13:24](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4404.6)):

Yes, correct.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:13:25](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4405.32)):

And how was it that you ended up being involved in the call at 47 Summer Street on January 24th?

Keith Nette ([01:13:32](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4412.55)):

We were recalled via a pager and text message that there was an incident going on and that we were to respond.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:13:39](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4419.69)):

So, can you explain to us how that works?

Keith Nette ([01:13:43](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4423.47)):

So,  a tone goes off through dispatch that sends off a pager at our houses  that alerts us. Then we also get text messages about the incident and  then respond either to the station or to the scene, whichever they  request.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:13:56](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4436.76)):

As a Duxbury firefighter, are part of your responsibilities to respond to these recalls?

Keith Nette ([01:14:06](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4446.12)):

Yes.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:14:06](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4446.81)):

Is it a voluntary thing?

Keith Nette ([01:14:08](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4448.19)):

Yes.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:14:08](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4448.55)):

So, if you weren't disposed or unavailable when a call went out or tone went out, would you be expected to respond to the scene?

Keith Nette ([01:14:16](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4456.05)):

No.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:14:16](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4456.77)):

But in this case, on January 24th, just after 6:00 PM, you heard that call go out, correct?

Keith Nette ([01:14:21](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4461.69)):

Correct.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:14:22](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4462.14)):

And so, then what did you do when you heard the call?

Keith Nette ([01:14:24](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4464.3)):

I drove to the scene in my personal truck.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:14:27](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4467.18)):

When you got to the scene, what did you see?

Keith Nette ([01:14:29](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4469.43)):

I saw police and fire vehicles. Then I walked up the driveway and walked into the back of the ambulance.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:14:37](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4477.86)):

Now, when you arrived and saw police, that was Duxbury Police?

Keith Nette ([01:14:42](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4482)):

Correct.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:14:42](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4482.6)):

And the fire personnel that was on scene, who was on scene, if you remember?

Keith Nette ([01:14:48](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4488.75)):

I only saw two people on scene initially. That was Kevin Heath and Sergeant Homestead.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:14:55](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4495.95)):

Okay. And Sergeant Homestead is a Duxbury officer?

Keith Nette ([01:14:57](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4497.15)):

Correct.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:14:58](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4498.08)):

Now, was there a Duxbury ambulance in the driveway when you pulled up?

Keith Nette ([01:15:01](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4501.38)):

Yes.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:15:02](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4502.1)):

And before even getting past that ambulance, were you asked to assist?

Keith Nette ([01:15:07](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4507.83)):

Yes.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:15:08](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4508.58)):

What did you see when you came to the back of that ambulance?

Keith Nette ([01:15:11](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4511.82)):

I saw an officer, Sergeant Homestead and Kevin Heath doing CPR on a child.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:15:23](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4523.1)):

Had you seen how the child ended up in the ambulance or did you come after?

Keith Nette ([01:15:26](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4526.79)):

I came after.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:15:27](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4527.54)):

Okay. Did you then help take over with treating that child in the back of the ambulance?

Keith Nette ([01:15:33](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4533.87)):

Yes.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:15:35](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4535.34)):

What was your initial observation of the patient? What did he look like?

Keith Nette ([01:15:40](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4540.59)):

Blue, white, non-responsive, not breathing, no pulse.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:15:46](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4546.08)):

And this particular patient, did you come to learn him to be the child named Dawson Clancy?

Keith Nette ([01:15:51](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4551.27)):

Yes.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:15:51](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4551.78)):

And he was approximately three years old?

Keith Nette ([01:15:53](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4553.55)):

Yes.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:15:54](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4554.96)):

What kind of treatment did you and the paramedic Heath offer to Dawson in the back of the ambulance? What did you do?

Keith Nette ([01:16:03](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4563.45)):

We  tried to start an IV. Then we also started an IO, which is kind of like  an IV. Tried intubation, which is an advanced airway, medications and  continued CPR.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:16:16](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4576.35)):

So, an IV, that's just putting a needle into somebody and trying to pump fluids in medicine, correct?

Keith Nette ([01:16:25](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4585.38)):

Correct.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:16:25](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4585.86)):

Tell us what an IO is.

Keith Nette ([01:16:27](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4587.42)):

An IO is interosseous, which is basically into the bone marrow on the lower leg.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:16:32](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4592.79)):

So, what do you do to try to enter an IO?

Keith Nette ([01:16:36](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4596.75)):

Usually we have a drill, but we had a manual one instead.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:16:42](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4602.39)):

So, what do you do with the manual drill to enter the IO?

Keith Nette ([01:16:46](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4606.08)):

You just twist and then it goes into the bone.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:16:48](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4608.96)):

And what bone do you attempt to do this in? And were you able to that IO into Dawson?

Keith Nette ([01:17:00](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4620.12)):

Yes.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:17:01](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4621.83)):

What is the purpose of the IO versus the IV?

Keith Nette ([01:17:05](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4625.91)):

If the patient has difficult access, which pediatrics do.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:17:10](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4630.2)):

And in this circumstance, what kinds of medication were you trying to deliver through the IO to Dawson?

Keith Nette ([01:17:17](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4637.31)):

Epinephrine.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:17:18](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4638.42)):

What's the purpose of epinephrine?

Keith Nette ([01:17:20](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4640.1)):

Basically just kind of helps to restart the heart.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:17:24](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4644.3)):

And at this point, he had no heart activity?

Keith Nette ([01:17:27](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4647.21)):

Correct.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:17:28](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4648.14)):

You also mentioned attempts to intubate. Can you tell us what that means?

Keith Nette ([01:17:32](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4652.1)):

So, intubation is an advanced airway where you put a tube into the airway to secure it to deliver high flow oxygen.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:17:40](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4660.74)):

Were you able to intubate Dawson?

Keith Nette ([01:17:43](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4663.29)):

Yes, Kevin Heath did.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:17:45](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4665.3)):

And what do you do when you enter the tool to intubate?

Keith Nette ([01:17:51](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4671.03)):

You go into the mouth and you do a motion of pulling up to see the vocal cords so you can pass the tube through.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:17:59](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4679.1)):

And what do you attach to the tube to try to increase the breath?

Keith Nette ([01:18:03](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4683.48)):

Oxygen and a BVM, which is basically you're squeezing a bag.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:18:07](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4687.86)):

So, BVM would stand for bag valve mask?

Keith Nette ([01:18:11](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4691.04)):

Correct.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:18:13](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4693.44)):

While  you were treating Dawson and doing the airway, intubating him, giving  him the medicines, were you able to observe anything about the area of  his neck?

Keith Nette ([01:18:26](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4706.43)):

Yes.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:18:27](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4707.75)):

What did you observe about his neck?

Keith Nette ([01:18:30](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4710.87)):

Dark red marks around his neck.

Ms. Buckingham ([01:18:37](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4717.62)):

Now, all of this is happening while you were in the back of the ambulance with Firefighter Heath, correct?

Keith Nette ([01:18:45](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=4725.93)):

Correct.

And did you follow?

And what did you see in the backyard?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:37:57](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5877.9)):

Came  to the backyard, saw firefighter Dan Doherty kneeling next to a person.  It was very dark. Ended up being Ms. Clancy. That was all we saw for a  few seconds.

Speaker 3 ([01:38:10](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5890.44)):

And at that point, did you hear something?

We did. Heard a voice yelling for us to get inside of the house, that there was a kid hanging in the basement.

Speaker 3 ([01:38:18](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5898.15)):

We did.

And what entrance did you go in?

Through the slider doors in the rear.

Speaker 3 ([01:38:24](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5904.15)):

Yes.

Yes.

Yes.

Speaker 3 ([01:38:35](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5915.97)):

Which direction did you go?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:38:37](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5917.11)):

We took a left.

Speaker 3 ([01:38:38](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5918.34)):

And what did you see when you went left?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:38:41](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5921.49)):

We took a left and we saw Callan and Cora face up on the ground.

Speaker 3 ([01:38:47](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5927.52)):

And when you saw them, what did you do?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:38:50](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5930.58)):

I  immediately jumped on the radio to ask for two additional ambulances,  that we needed more help. And Patrick ran to Cora and I went to Callan.

Speaker 3 ([01:39:01](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5941.08)):

And showing you what's been marked as Exhibit 100. Is this the room that you entered?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:39:24](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5964.45)):

Yes.

Speaker 3 ([01:39:25](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5965.47)):

And where was Cora, to your memory?

Cora was just to the left in this picture, and Callan was just to the right.

Speaker 3 ([01:39:35](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5975.97)):

And when you say just to the left, are you putting over towards the equipment against the wall in the picture?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:39:41](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5981.64)):

Yes.

Speaker 3 ([01:39:42](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5982.48)):

And when you say to the right, are you pointing to the area to the right of the dresser?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:39:47](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5987.55)):

Yes.

Speaker 3 ([01:39:48](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5988)):

I'm showing you Exhibit 98. Is that the area where Callan was found?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:39:56](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5996.88)):

Yes.

Speaker 3 ([01:39:58](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=5998.08)):

And close up Exhibit 98 nine, do you see the pajamas there, the stethoscope and the blue exercise bands?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:40:04](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6004.98)):

Yes.

Speaker 3 ([01:40:05](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6005.43)):

Do you recall removing Callan's pajamas to treat him?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:40:11](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6011.52)):

I did, yes.

Speaker 3 ([01:40:17](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6017.67)):

So, when you went over to Callan, you remove his clothing, what do you do after that?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:40:23](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6023.13)):

I  didn't remove his entire onesie right away, checked him for a pulse. He  did not have one. Immediately started chest compressions on him.

Speaker 3 ([01:40:32](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6032.49)):

Now this is an eight-month-old baby, correct?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:40:35](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6035.04)):

Yes.

Speaker 3 ([01:40:35](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6035.79)):

Is the way you do chest compressions for a baby different than other people, other ages?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:40:40](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6040.74)):

Yes, because of the size, you have to scoop your arms around and you compress with your thumbs in the center.

Speaker 4 ([01:40:44](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6044.55)):

I'll allow it.

Speaker 3 ([01:40:49](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6049.26)):

So,  you said you have to put your hands underneath on the back of the child  and then use your thumbs to do compressions? Yes. And is that what you  did for Callan?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:40:56](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6056.13)):

Yes.

Speaker 3 ([01:40:57](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6057.48)):

And what did you do next?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:41:00](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6060.09)):

Continued  CPR on him until more help and hands started to arrive on scene. We had  an off-duty firefighter, Tom Brandt, come in and he started to assist  me. He took over compressions briefly while more help started to arrive.

Speaker 3 ([01:41:15](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6075.48)):

When Brandt took over chest compressions, what did you do?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:41:19](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6079.71)):

At  that point, someone had brought down a bag valve mask to help us  deliver oxygen to the child, and we started delivering oxygen to the  child.

Speaker 3 ([01:41:29](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6089.52)):

And  at some point, did you remove the pajamas completely? Yes. And when you  removed the pajamas completely, was that so someone could attempt to  get IO access through the baby's legs? And was that successful?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:41:51](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6111.12)):

That was not successful, no.

Speaker 3 ([01:41:52](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6112.41)):

Is that difficult to do with a child that age?

Jennifer Stratton ([01:41:56](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6116.22)):

It's a little more difficult. It's a less frequent practice skill.

I attempted once and another provider attempted a second time. I don't remember who that was.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:46:07](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6367.14)):

Okay. And did you respond to the area of 47 Summer Street?

Robert Costanzo ([01:46:10](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6370.92)):

Yes, we did.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:46:11](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6371.79)):

And approximately how long did it take you to get there?

Robert Costanzo ([01:46:15](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6375.48)):

Between five and eight minutes.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:46:17](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6377.7)):

When you arrived, what did you see?

Robert Costanzo ([01:46:20](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6380.49)):

There were multiple pieces of equipment, other ambulances, police cars.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:46:26](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6386.67)):

When you arrived, were you directed to the backyard?

Robert Costanzo ([01:46:30](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6390.06)):

Yes.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:46:31](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6391.44)):

And did you see a patient there in the backyard?

Robert Costanzo ([01:46:34](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6394.29)):

Yes, we did.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:46:35](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6395.1)):

And where was she located?

Robert Costanzo ([01:46:37](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6397.08)):

She  was on the ground with a Duxbury firefighter. She was already C-spined  on a long board with a collar. She was unresponsive, but breathing on  the ground.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:46:45](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6405.84)):

And did you learn her name was Lindsay Clancy?

Robert Costanzo ([01:46:47](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6407.73)):

Yes.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:46:48](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6408.48)):

And  when you say she was already on a board with a C-spine, what type of  equipment are we talking about? And if you could just explain to the  jurors.

Robert Costanzo ([01:46:55](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6415.83)):

So  it's just a long flat board that we use to secure people when there's  possible spinal injury. And she already had the collar on her neck. They  had already started treatment prior to our arrival, so they had her all  secured to the long backboard with the collar applied.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:47:13](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6433.92)):

And did you notice anything about her wrists at that time?

Robert Costanzo ([01:47:16](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6436.35)):

Yes.  Her wrists were bandaged previously, assuming some kind of injury.  There was no active bleeding or anything through the bandage, but her  wrists were bandaged.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:47:27](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6447.39)):

Was it white bandaging?

Robert Costanzo ([01:47:27](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6447.66)):

Yes.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:47:29](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6449.37)):

And so nothing was seeping through to your eye? Did you see anything on her neck area?

Robert Costanzo ([01:47:35](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6455.73)):

She  had some superficial lacerations that we could see around the collar,  but we don't typically remove equipment after it's already been placed.  So I didn't investigate any further than that. There was no active  bleeding that I could see, so we just left it alone.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:47:50](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6470.07)):

So even with the collar, there was no blood flowing out of the collar or pooling around her or anything like that?

Robert Costanzo ([01:47:55](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6475.29)):

Correct.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:47:58](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6478.92)):

Was she breathing?

Robert Costanzo ([01:47:59](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6479.73)):

She was.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:48:00](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6480.66)):

Was she responsive?

Robert Costanzo ([01:48:01](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6481.83)):

No.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:48:02](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6482.67)):

Was she making any noise at all?

Robert Costanzo ([01:48:04](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6484.44)):

She was moaning and groaning, but no words, no verbal response.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:48:10](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6490.05)):

Did you bring her to your ambulance?

Robert Costanzo ([01:48:11](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6491.61)):

Yes, we did.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:48:13](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6493.23)):

And when you got to the ambulance, what was your role?

Robert Costanzo ([01:48:18](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6498.72)):

I  was the tech in charge. So we did as much of an assessment as we could  without removing her from the board to look for any further injuries.  Other than the injuries that the Duxbury Fire Department had already  found, we didn't find any further injuries. And then we continued with  treatment, vital signs and such.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:48:38](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6518.52)):

When you did the assessment, did you look at her head?

Robert Costanzo ([01:48:41](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6521.04)):

Yes.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:48:41](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6521.61)):

Were there any cuts or bumps or dents that you noticed in her head?

Robert Costanzo ([01:48:44](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6524.58)):

Not that we noticed, no.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:48:46](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6526.47)):

What  about her arms? Other than the bandaging on her wrist, were there any  cuts, obvious broken bones or injuries to her arms or her hands?

Robert Costanzo ([01:48:56](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6536.91)):

No obvious broken bones or anything, no.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:49:03](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6543.3)):

And you said you were the tech. What does that mean?

Robert Costanzo ([01:49:06](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6546.03)):

I  was the medic that was in charge. [inaudible 01:49:13] medic that's in  charge or you're the medic in charge. So I was the medic in charge.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:49:16](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6556.83)):

And was anyone else in the back of the ambulance with you treating Ms. Clancy?

Robert Costanzo ([01:49:20](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6560.28)):

Yes, my partner, Dan Smith.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:49:21](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6561.87)):

Who was driving the ambulance, if you recall?

Robert Costanzo ([01:49:23](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6563.91)):

It was our deputy chief, Jim Shea.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:49:26](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6566.55)):

And do you recall which hospital you went to?

Robert Costanzo ([01:49:30](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6570.87)):

South Shore Hospital.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:49:33](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6573.75)):

Well, let me ask you, how long approximately did it take you to get from 47 Summer Street in Duxbury to South Shore Hospital?

Robert Costanzo ([01:49:42](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6582.27)):

It's  typically about, from Pembroke, it would be about 15 minutes, so it  would be a little bit longer than that. I'm not sure of the exact time,  so probably maybe 18 minutes.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:49:51](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6591.48)):

Okay.  And while you're in that transport in the back of the ambulance, after  you've done this examination of Lindsay Clancy to see if there are any  other injuries, what do you do next?

Robert Costanzo ([01:50:03](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6603.57)):

So  we would start treatment. So we put in two IVs as a precaution and any  other treatment that was needed. So we noticed that her pupils were  constricted and she was unresponsive. So we treated for a potential  opioid overdose. We gave two milligrams of Narcan and she seemed to  become a little bit more agitated, reaching, trying to grab, reaching  out with her arms, but no verbal communication. She never fully woke up.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:50:38](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6638.28)):

And have you given Narcan to individuals in the past in your role as a paramedic?

Robert Costanzo ([01:50:44](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6644.76)):

Yes, I have.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:50:45](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6645.33)):

What is the typical reaction when you give a patient Narcan?

Robert Costanzo ([01:50:49](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6649.35)):

It  depends on the drug that they took or the amount of drugs that they  took, but depending on what they take, their respirations will go up,  their pupils will get a little bigger. Sometimes they fully wake up.

Kevin Reddington ([01:51:01](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6661.86)):

Can we know who they are? Who's referred to?

Robert Costanzo ([01:51:02](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6662.4)):

The patient.

Kevin Reddington ([01:51:02](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6662.4)):

Yeah. All right.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:51:07](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6667.5)):

And you were referring to patients that you've dealt with in the past in your role as a paramedic, is that correct?

Robert Costanzo ([01:51:11](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6671.37)):

That's correct, yes.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:51:14](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6674.31)):

Okay. And so they would often wake up when given Narcan?

Robert Costanzo ([01:51:16](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6676.95)):

Yes.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:51:18](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6678.99)):

And what type of drugs, based on your training experience, does Narcan work on?

Robert Costanzo ([01:51:24](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6684)):

We're trained to use it for opioid overdoses. So heroin, fentanyl, other drugs like that.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:51:30](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6690.57)):

Okay. And does Narcan typically work on benzodiazepine?

Robert Costanzo ([01:51:35](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6695.25)):

We are not trained to use it for anything other than opioids.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:51:38](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6698.55)):

Okay. And when you gave Lindsay Clancy the Narcan, she did not wake up, correct?

Robert Costanzo ([01:51:43](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6703.92)):

No.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:51:46](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6706.11)):

Did you provide any other care to Lindsay Clancy on the way to the hospital?

Robert Costanzo ([01:51:50](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6710.94)):

Just continued to monitor her vitals and make sure there was no decline.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:51:54](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6714.93)):

And once you arrived at the hospital, did you transfer her care to the hospital staff?

Robert Costanzo ([01:51:59](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=6719.88)):

Yes, we did.

Yes. Yes.

Shanan Buckingham ([01:59:59](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=7199.28)):

Shanan Buckingham ([02:00:21](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=7221.18)):

And what is painful stimuli?

Shanan Buckingham ([02:00:27](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=7227.69)):

And painful stimuli, is that when someone reacts physically or verbally to pain?

Yes.

Judge William Sullivan ([02:13:25](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=8005.02)):

Counsel?

Kevin Reddington ([02:13:25](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=8005.65)):

No.

Judge William Sullivan ([02:13:26](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=8006.01)):

All right. Thank you, sir. Be safe.

Vincent Cahill ([02:13:27](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=8007.12)):

Thank you, Your Honor.

Judge William Sullivan ([02:13:27](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=8007.51)):

Thank you.

Shanan Buckingham ([02:13:28](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=8008.71)):

Your Honor, if we may approach sidebar.

Judge William Sullivan ([02:13:29](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=8009.85)):

Sure.  All right. Members of the jury, talking to counsel, there still is a  couple of issues that we have to hammer out and get done. And rather  than have you wait around, I'm not quite sure how long that's going to  go, I'm going to excuse you for the day. All right? As I told you,  sometimes the schedules don't always match up what I tell you they're  going to do even a couple hours before. So I'm going to excuse you until  tomorrow morning. All right? And so tomorrow morning, the plan is that  we're going to go on this view. And I'll explain it a little bit more in  detail tomorrow, but just so you can picture it in your head, I'm going  to ask you to be here, to be ready to come out here at nine o'clock.  I'm going to address you, probably ask you those same questions.

([02:14:22](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=8062.92))
Then  I'll give you a little bit of information about the view. I'll give you  an instruction. What the view is, we're going to go to a couple of  areas. You're going to be able to get out and look at these areas. The  lawyers will be there. They'll be able to point out what they feel is  significant. And at that point, we'll then come back. We'll continue  with some of the testimony. We may end up a little bit early tomorrow as  well in regards to the schedules. And at that point, I'm going to talk  to counsel, see where we are in regards to the overall schedule. But  It's my impression that we are ahead of schedule at this point. So I  just wanted to let you know that. So I'm going to excuse you till  tomorrow morning rather than have you sit here and just wait in regards  to that.

([02:15:11](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=8111.52))
So  I'm going to excuse you till tomorrow. I'd ask you to be here at 9:00,  check the weather, see, but you'll be on the view in the morning and  then there'll be some witnesses in the afternoon. All right. So remember  I'm going to ask you those questions. Don't read anything, don't talk  about it, don't do any research, don't take any field trips, and I'll  see you tomorrow morning. Okay? Thank you.

Speaker 9 ([02:15:36](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=8136.45)):

Court,  all rise. Jurors, close your notebooks, place them on the chairs,  please. Follow me. Jurors have exited the floor. This court's in  session.

Judge William Sullivan ([02:16:14](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=8174.1)):

All  right. In recess until tomorrow, the media the court had issued  previously a specific order regarding the view. There was a supplemental  order issued this morning, so everybody should just be aware of that.  And I want to thank everybody for the consideration and that will be in  recess until tomorrow morning.

Speaker 9 ([02:16:39](https://www.rev.com/app/transcript/NmE2ZTBkYmU3OWFjZmJmNWUyMWUyYmE2WjlGUkdlYk1tRFlW/o/VEMwMzI1ODM0MTM3?ts=8199.33)):
